Eight RFS documents: what repeats, what changes

Each letter is one document’s existing DITA node in that row. Green enclosures group exact expanded content; dashed single nodes differ. Gray groups are empty headings. Grouping describes content, not authorization to use one instruction for every RFS subject.

F and S are display labels. Their source titles both use B. Scope: eight English task maps from en_EN; this is not a task-versus-activity comparison.

How to read the evidence

Rows align by heading path, with the two linking-title variants explicitly aligned. Shared notes align by source ID, not by generic “Note” titles or ordinal position. This avoids inventing correspondence between unrelated callouts; different note IDs appear on separate rows and may still repeat wording across rows.

Exact groups require reference-expanded body structure and link targets to match. Nonexact pair labels reuse the earlier deterministic audit: high lexical overlap with changed fields/links is a variant candidate; an identical internal XML block of at least 12 tokens supports partial overlap; remaining pairs stay related/unresolved. No semantic equivalence is confirmed, no LLM/model inference is run, and similarity edges are never transitively merged. Pair labels apply only within aligned rows; cross-row note duplicates are listed in the companion narrative.

1. SummaryCEGKMNFS

8 document occurrences form 8 exact-content group(s): C | E | G | K | M | N | F | S. Exact means the expanded body, element order, attributes and link targets match after case/whitespace normalization; it does not establish equal applicability.

Pair evidence: Variant candidate 28. These labels are deterministic triage, not approved consolidation decisions.

The common trigger is an ROE that cannot be linked or whose adjudication need cannot be determined. The RFS category and Fishing context change. Treat this as a template family with explicit subject values, not one unqualified summary.

All eight publications are business-type task documents. Their RFS subject is inherited even when this section contains no category name. Exact text is a review opportunity, not permission to erase that scope.

C · Linking the ROE and the matching period of employment · 579 tokens

en_EN/task/roe_rts/linking_the_roe_and_the_matching_period_f71580ff.dita

Full expanded section

When an ROE RFS Action WI is received and a link has not yet been created between the ROE and a period of employment, the Non-complex agent must review the applicable ROE in RCM and the periods of employment listed in NWS. The Non-complex agent determines if one of the periods of employment listed in NWS matches the employer’s name on the ROE. Variations in the employer’s name Minor spelling differences between the employer’s name on the ROE and the period of employment are accepted. The agent must also consider that the employer may do business under more than one name. For example, the ROE indicates that the employer’s name is 123456 ONT Inc., but the client reported working for ABC Company. If the documentation on file or the Employer search (by business number) in FTS indicates that 123456 ONT Inc. operates as ABC Company, the names are equivalent and considered to be a match. Reviewing the dates on the ROE Only one ROE can be matched to each period of employment listed in NWS. The agent must review the Search / Correct - Results screen in RCM to determine if the ROE in the ROE RFS Action WI is the one with the last day for which paid (LDP) closest to the last day worked (LDW) for one of the periods of employment. The agent creates a link between the ROE and the period of employment with the matching employer’s name and best match for LDP and LDW. If either the employer’s name does not match or the LDP and LDW are not a good match, the agent does not create a link between the ROE and the period of employment. More than one ROE for one period of employment There may be more than one ROE but only one period of employment for the same employer. Example 1 Period of employment information declared on application: Callout Search / Correct - Results in RCM: SN Employer FDW / TSD LDP / TED Modified Input RFS Audit EHF W12345678 ABC Company 01/10/2015 04/12/2015 22/12/2015 22/12/2015 C W12341234 ABC Company 01/01/2014 28/08/2015 22/12/2015 22/12/2015 C ROE RFS Action WI #1 for W12345678 The agent completes the period of employment information section as follows: Matching POE : Select ABC Company . ROE RFS Action WI #2 for W12341234 The agent completes the period of employment information section as follows: Matching POE : Select POE not in the list . There may also be more than one period of employment for the same employer. Example 2 Period of employment information declared on application: Callout Callout Search / Correct - Results in RCM: SN Employer FDW / TSD LDP / TED Modified Input RFS Audit EHF W12345678 ABC Company 01/10/2015 04/12/2015 22/12/2015 22/12/2015 C W12341234 ABC Company 01/01/2014 28/08/2015 22/12/2015 22/12/2015 C ROE RFS Action WI #1 for W12345678 The agent completes the period of employment information section as follows: Matching POE : Select ABC Company (LDW 30/11/2015). ROE RFS Action WI #2 for W12341234 The agent completes the period of employment information section as follows: Matching POE : Select ABC Company (LDW 14/08/2015). ROE already linked If the ROE is linked to a period of employment with a corresponding electronic record of decision (ROD) or an outstanding Adjudication Issue (ADJ) WI, the ROE RFS Action WI is completed without further action by the Non-complex agent.

Original XML

<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<!DOCTYPE concept PUBLIC "-//OASIS//DTD DITA 2.0 Concept//EN" "concept.dtd">
<concept id="linking_the_roe_and_the_matching_period_f71580ff">
  <title>Linking the ROE and the matching period of employment</title>
  <conbody>
    <p>When an ROE RFS Action WI is received and a link has not yet been created between the ROE and a period of employment, the Non-complex agent must review the applicable ROE in RCM and the periods of employment listed in NWS. The Non-complex agent determines if one of the periods of employment listed in NWS matches the employer’s name on the ROE.</p>
    <section>
      <title>Variations in the employer’s name</title>
    </section>
    <p>Minor spelling differences between the employer’s name on the ROE and the period of employment are accepted. The agent must also consider that the employer may do business under more than one name. For example, the ROE indicates that the employer’s name is 123456 ONT Inc., but the client reported working for ABC Company. If the documentation on file or the Employer search (by business number) in FTS indicates that 123456 ONT Inc. operates as ABC Company, the names are equivalent and considered to be a match.</p>
    <section>
      <title>Reviewing the dates on the ROE</title>
    </section>
    <p>Only one ROE can be matched to each period of employment listed in NWS. The agent must review the Search / Correct - Results screen in RCM to determine if the ROE in the ROE RFS Action WI is the one with the last day for which paid (LDP) closest to the last day worked (LDW) for one of the periods of employment. The agent creates a link between the ROE and the period of employment with the matching employer’s name and best match for LDP and LDW. If either the employer’s name does not match or the LDP and LDW are not a good match, the agent does not create a link between the ROE and the period of employment.</p>
    <section>
      <title>More than one ROE for one period of employment</title>
    </section>
    <p>There may be more than one ROE but only one period of employment for the same employer.</p>
    <section>
      <title>Example 1</title>
    </section>
    <ul>
      <li>
        Period of employment information declared on application:
        <xref href="../../common_notes/note_c_4692.dita">Callout</xref>
      </li>
      <li>
        Search / Correct - Results in RCM:
        <table conref="../../common_tables/table_c_0621.dita#table_c_0621/body"/>
      </li>
      <li>
        ROE RFS Action WI #1 for W12345678
        <ul>
          <li>
            The agent completes the period of employment information section as follows:
            <ul>
              <li>Matching POE : Select ABC Company .</li>
            </ul>
          </li>
        </ul>
      </li>
      <li>
        ROE RFS Action WI #2 for W12341234
        <ul>
          <li>
            The agent completes the period of employment information section as follows:
            <ul>
              <li>Matching POE : Select POE not in the list .</li>
            </ul>
          </li>
        </ul>
      </li>
    </ul>
    <p>There may also be more than one period of employment for the same employer.</p>
    <section>
      <title>Example 2</title>
    </section>
    <ul>
      <li>
        Period of employment information declared on application:
        <xref href="../../common_notes/note_c_4693.dita">Callout</xref>
        <xref href="../../common_notes/note_c_4694.dita">Callout</xref>
      </li>
      <li>
        Search / Correct - Results in RCM:
        <table conref="../../common_tables/table_c_0621.dita#table_c_0621/body"/>
      </li>

        <ul>

    <section>
      <title>ROE already linked</title>
    </section>
    <p>If the ROE is linked to a period of employment with a corresponding electronic record of decision (ROD) or an outstanding Adjudication Issue (ADJ) WI, the ROE RFS Action WI is completed without further action by the Non-complex agent.</p>
  </conbody>

M · Linking the ROE and the matching period of employmen · 579 tokens

en_EN/task/roe_dismissal/linking_the_roe_and_the_matching_period_e21c3afb.dita

Full expanded section

When an ROE RFS Action WI is received and a link has not yet been created between the ROE and a period of employment, the Non-complex agent must review the applicable ROE in RCM and the periods of employment listed in NWS. The Non-complex agent determines if one of the periods of employment listed in NWS matches the employer’s name on the ROE. Variations in the employer’s name Minor spelling differences between the employer’s name on the ROE and the period of employment are accepted. The agent must also consider that the employer may do business under more than one name. For example, the ROE indicates that the employer’s name is 123456 ONT Inc., but the client reported working for ABC Company. If the documentation on file or the Employer search (by business number) in FTS indicates that 123456 ONT Inc. operates as ABC Company, the names are equivalent and considered to be a match. Reviewing the dates on the ROE Only one ROE can be matched to each period of employment listed in NWS. The agent must review the Search / Correct - Results screen in RCM to determine if the ROE in the ROE RFS Action WI is the one with the last day for which paid (LDP) closest to the last day worked (LDW) for one of the periods of employment. The agent creates a link between the ROE and the period of employment with the matching employer’s name and best match for LDP and LDW. If either the employer’s name does not match or the LDP and LDW are not a good match, the agent does not create a link between the ROE and the period of employment. More than one ROE for one period of employment There may be more than one ROE but only one period of employment for the same employer. Example 1 Period of employment information declared on application: Callout Search / Correct - Results in RCM: SN Employer FDW / TSD LDP / TED Modified Input RFS Audit EHF W12345678 ABC Company 01/07/2015 04/12/2015 22/12/2015 22/12/2015 M W12341234 ABC Company 01/01/2015 30/06/2015 22/12/2015 22/12/2015 K ROE RFS Action WI #1 for W12345678 The agent completes the period of employment information section as follows: Matching POE : Select ABC Company . ROE RFS Action WI #2 for W12341234 The agent completes the period of employment information section as follows: Matching POE : Select POE not in the list . There may also be more than one period of employment for the same employer. Example 2 Period of employment information declared on application: Callout Callout Search / Correct - Results in RCM: SN Employer FDW / TSD LDP / TED Modified Input RFS Audit EHF W12345678 ABC Company 01/07/2015 04/12/2015 22/12/2015 22/12/2015 M W12341234 ABC Company 01/01/2015 30/06/2015 22/12/2015 22/12/2015 K ROE RFS Action WI #1 for W12345678 The agent completes the period of employment information section as follows: Matching POE : Select ABC Company (LDW 30/11/2015). ROE RFS Action WI #2 for W12341234 The agent completes the period of employment information section as follows: Matching POE : Select ABC Company (LDW 30/07/2015). ROE already linked If the ROE is linked to a period of employment with a corresponding electronic record of decision (ROD) or an outstanding Adjudication Issue (ADJ) WI, the ROE RFS Action WI is completed without further action by the Non-complex agent.

  <title>Linking the ROE and the matching period of employmen</title>

    <p>When an ROE RFS Action WI is received and a link has not yet been created between the ROE and a period of employment, the Non-complex agent must review the applicable ROE in RCM and the periods of employment listed in NWS. The Non-complex agent determines if one of the periods of employment listed in NWS matches the employer’s name on the ROE.</p>
    <section>
      <title>Variations in the employer’s name</title>
    </section>
    <p>Minor spelling differences between the employer’s name on the ROE and the period of employment are accepted. The agent must also consider that the employer may do business under more than one name. For example, the ROE indicates that the employer’s name is 123456 ONT Inc., but the client reported working for ABC Company. If the documentation on file or the Employer search (by business number) in FTS indicates that 123456 ONT Inc. operates as ABC Company, the names are equivalent and considered to be a match.</p>

      <title>Reviewing the dates on the ROE</title>
    </section>
    <p>Only one ROE can be matched to each period of employment listed in NWS. Example 1 Period of employment information declared on application: Callout Search / Correct - Results in RCM: SN Employer FDW / TSD LDP / TED Modified Input RFS Audit EHF W12345678 ABC Company 01/07/2015 04/12/2015 22/12/2015 22/12/2015 B W12341234 ABC Company 01/01/2015 30/06/2015 22/12/2015 22/12/2015 K ROE RFS Action WI #1 for W12345678 The agent completes the period of employment information section as follows: Matching POE : Select ABC Company . ROE RFS Action WI #2 for W12341234 The agent completes the period of employment information section as follows: Matching POE : Select POE not in the list . There may also be more than one period of employment for the same employer. Example 2 Period of employment information declared on application: Callout Callout Search / Correct - Results in RCM: SN Employer FDW / TSD LDP / TED Modified Input RFS Audit EHF W12345678 ABC Company 01/07/2015 04/12/2015 22/12/2015 22/12/2015 B W12341234 ABC Company 01/01/2015 30/06/2015 22/12/2015 22/12/2015 E ROE RFS Action WI #1 for W12345678 The agent completes the period of employment information section as follows: Matching POE : Select ABC Company (LDW 30/11/2015). ROE RFS Action WI #2 for W12341234 The agent completes the period of employment information section as follows: Matching POE : Select ABC Company (LDW 30/07/2015). ROE already linked If the ROE is linked to a period of employment with a corresponding electronic record of decision (ROD) or an outstanding Adjudication Issue (ADJ) WI, the ROE RFS Action WI is completed without further action by the Non-complex agent.

Original XML

<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<!DOCTYPE concept PUBLIC "-//OASIS//DTD DITA 2.0 Concept//EN" "concept.dtd">
<concept id="linking_the_roe_and_the_matching_period_f71580ff">
  <title>Linking the ROE and the matching period of employment</title>
  <conbody>
    <p>When an ROE RFS Action WI is received and a link has not yet been created between the ROE and a period of employment, the Non-complex agent must review the applicable ROE in RCM and the periods of employment listed in NWS. The Non-complex agent determines if one of the periods of employment listed in NWS matches the employer’s name on the ROE.</p>
    <section>
      <title>Variations in the employer’s name</title>
    </section>
    <p>Minor spelling differences between the employer’s name on the ROE and the period of employment are accepted. The agent must also consider that the employer may do business under more than one name. For example, the ROE indicates that the employer’s name is 123456 ONT Inc., but the client reported working for ABC Company. If the documentation on file or the Employer search (by business number) in FTS indicates that 123456 ONT Inc. operates as ABC Company, the names are equivalent and considered to be a match.</p>
    <section>
      <title>Reviewing the dates on the ROE</title>
    </section>
    <p>Only one ROE can be matched to each period of employment listed in NWS. The agent must review the Search / Correct - Results screen in RCM to determine if the ROE in the ROE RFS Action WI is the one with the last day for which paid (LDP) closest to the last day worked (LDW) for one of the periods of employment. The agent creates a link between the ROE and the period of employment with the matching employer’s name and best match for LDP and LDW. If either the employer’s name does not match or the LDP and LDW are not a good match, the agent does not create a link between the ROE and the period of employment.</p>
    <section>
      <title>More than one ROE for one period of employment</title>
    </section>
    <p>There may be more than one ROE but only one period of employment for the same employer.</p>
    <section>
      <title>Example 1</title>
    </section>
    <ul>
      <li>
        Period of employment information declared on application:
        <xref href="../../common_notes/note_c_2682.dita">Callout</xref>
      </li>
    </ul>
    <ul>
      <li>
        Search / Correct - Results in RCM:
        <table conref="../../common_tables/table_c_0507.dita#table_c_0507/body"/>
      </li>
      <li>
        ROE RFS Action WI #1 for W12345678
        <ul>
          <li>
            The agent completes the period of employment information section as follows:
            <ul>
              <li>Matching POE : Select ABC Company .</li>
            </ul>
          </li>
        </ul>
      </li>
      <li>
        ROE RFS Action WI #2 for W12341234
        <ul>
          <li>
            The agent completes the period of employment information section as follows:
            <ul>
              <li>Matching POE : Select POE not in the list .</li>
            </ul>
          </li>
        </ul>
      </li>
    </ul>
    <p>There may also be more than one period of employment for the same employer.</p>
    <section>
      <title>Example 2</title>
    </section>
    <ul>
      <li>
        Period of employment information declared on application:
        <xref href="../../common_notes/note_c_2683.dita">Callout</xref>
        <xref href="../../common_notes/note_c_2684.dita">Callout</xref>
      </li>
      <li>
        Search / Correct - Results in RCM:
        <table conref="../../common_tables/table_c_0508.dita#table_c_0508/body"/>
      </li>

        <ul>
          <li>
            The agent completes the period of employment information section as follows:

    <section>
      <title>ROE already linked</title>
    </section>
    <p>If the ROE is linked to a period of employment with a corresponding electronic record of decision (ROD) or an outstanding Adjudication Issue (ADJ) WI, the ROE RFS Action WI is completed without further action by the Non-complex agent.</p>

When an ROE RFS Action WI is received and a link has not yet been created between the ROE and a period of employment, the Non-complex agent must review the applicable ROE in RCM and the periods of employment listed in NWS. The Non-complex agent determines if one of the periods of employment listed in NWS matches the employer’s name on the ROE.

M–S · Variant candidate

Token Jaccard 0.980892; exact internal blocks ≥12 tokens: 6. Signals: reference_targets|inherited_context_difference

Only one ROE can be matched to each period of employment listed in NWS. The agent must review the Search / Correct - Results screen in RCM to determine if the ROE in the ROE RFS Action WI is the one with the last day for which paid (LDP) closest to the last day worked (LDW) for one of the periods of employment. The agent creates a link between the ROE and the period of employment with the matching employer’s name and best match for LDP and LDW. If either the employer’s name does not match or the LDP and LDW are not a good match, the agent does not create a link between the ROE and the period of employment.

C · Completing the Resolve Issue section · 183 tokens

en_EN/task/roe_rts/completing_the_resolve_issue_section.dita

Full expanded section

If the ROE is not linked to a period of employment or if the ROE is linked to a period of employment without a corresponding electronic ROD or ADJ WI, the agent must complete the Resolve Issue section of the ROE RFS Action WI before completing the WI. When resolving the issue, the agent must review the ROE and the existing RODs. Comments in block 18 If the ROE was issued with comments in block 18 , the agent must determine if the correct RFS is indicated on the ROE. Any required changes to the RFS details must be made in the Resolve Issue tab of the ROE RFS Action WI. Note Reviewing existing records of decision (RODs) on file If the RFS is contentious and there is an existing ROD, the agent must determine if the information on the ROE contradicts the decision on file. When a ROD is not found or when the information on the ROE contradicts the ROD, the agent indicates that no decision matches the ROE when completing the Resolve Issue section of the ROE RFS Action WI. Note

Original XML

<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<!DOCTYPE concept PUBLIC "-//OASIS//DTD DITA 2.0 Concept//EN" "concept.dtd">
<concept id="completing_the_resolve_issue_section">
  <title>Completing the Resolve Issue section</title>
  <conbody>
    <p>If the ROE is not linked to a period of employment or if the ROE is linked to a period of employment without a corresponding electronic ROD or ADJ WI, the agent must complete the Resolve Issue section of the ROE RFS Action WI before completing the WI. When resolving the issue, the agent must review the ROE and the existing RODs.</p>
    <section>
      <title>Comments in block 18</title>
    </section>
    <p>If the ROE was issued with comments in block 18 , the agent must determine if the correct RFS is indicated on the ROE. Any required changes to the RFS details must be made in the Resolve Issue tab of the ROE RFS Action WI.</p>
    <xref href="../../common_notes/note_c_4683.dita">Note</xref>
    <section>
      <title>Reviewing existing records of decision (RODs) on file</title>
    </section>
    <p>If the RFS is contentious and there is an existing ROD, the agent must determine if the information on the ROE contradicts the decision on file. When a ROD is not found or when the information on the ROE contradicts the ROD, the agent indicates that no decision matches the ROE when completing the Resolve Issue section of the ROE RFS Action WI.</p>
    <xref href="../../common_notes/note_c_2675.dita">Note</xref>
  </conbody>
</concept>

en_EN/task/roe_quit/completing_the_resolve_issue_section.dita

Full expanded section

If the ROE is not linked to a period of employment or if the ROE is linked to a period of employment without a corresponding electronic ROD or ADJ WI, the agent must complete the Resolve Issue section of the ROE RFS Action WI before completing the WI. When resolving the issue, the agent must review the ROE and the existing RODs. Comments in block 18 If the ROE was issued with comments in block 18 , the agent must determine if the correct RFS is indicated on the ROE. Any required changes to the RFS details must be made in the Resolve Issue tab of the ROE RFS Action WI. Note Reviewing existing records of decision (RODs) on file If the Resolve Issue section is displayed and there is an existing ROD, the agent must determine if the information on the ROE contradicts the decision on file. When a ROD is not found or when the information on the ROE contradicts the ROD, the agent indicates that no decision matches the ROE when completing the Resolve Issue section of the ROE RFS Action WI. M · Completing the Resolve Issue section · 236 tokens

en_EN/task/roe_dismissal/completing_the_resolve_issue_section.dita

Full expanded section

If the ROE is not linked to a period of employment or if the ROE is linked to a period of employment without a corresponding electronic ROD or ADJ WI, the agent must complete the Resolve Issue section of the ROE RFS Action WI before completing the WI. When resolving the issue, the agent must review the ROE and the existing RODs. Comments in block 18 If the ROE was issued with comments in block 18 , the agent must determine if the correct RFS is indicated on the ROE. Any required changes to the RFS details must be made in the Resolve Issue section of the ROE RFS Action WI. Note Reviewing existing records of decision (RODs) on file If the Resolve Issue section is displayed and there is an existing ROD, the agent must determine if the information on the ROE contradicts the decision on file. If the ROD on file indicates Dismissal — ROE confirmation required , the agent must confirm that the comments on the ROE indicate one of the following: The client was dismissed due to unsuitability. The client did not pass the probationary period. The client was terminated during the probationary period. When a ROD is not found or when the information on the ROE contradicts or does not confirm the ROD, the agent indicates that no decision matches the ROE when completing the Resolve Issue section of the ROE RFS Action WI. Note

F · Completing the Resolve Issue section · 216 tokens

en_EN/task/completing_roe_fishing_roe/completing_the_resolve_issue_section.dita

Full expanded section

If the ROE is not linked to a period of employment or if the ROE is linked to a period of employment without a corresponding electronic ROD or ADJ WI, the agent must complete the Resolve Issue section of the ROE RFS Action WI before completing the WI. When resolving the issue, the agent must review the ROE and the existing RODs. Comments in block 18 If the ROE was issued with comments in block 12 , the agent must determine if the correct RFS is indicated on the ROE. Any required changes to the RFS details must be made in the Resolve Issue section of the ROE RFS Action WI. Note Reason for separation (RFS) options on fishing ROEs There are only 2 RFS options on a paper fishing ROE: A - End of fishing and B - Other . There are 3 RFS options on a Web fishing ROE: A - End of fishing, A - Requested by Fisher and B - Other . In RCM, all 3 options are available when inputting a fishing ROE. There is no Special Condition option on a fishing ROE. Reviewing existing records of decision (RODs) on file If there is an existing ROD indicating that the RFS is contentious, the agent must not modify the RFS details when completing the Resolve Issue section of the ROE RFS Action WI. Note

S · Completing the Resolve Issue section · 194 tokens

en_EN/task/completing_roe_strike_lockout/completing_the_resolve_issue_section.dita

Full expanded section

If the ROE is not linked to a period of employment or if the ROE is linked to a period of employment without a corresponding electronic ROD or ADJ WI, the agent must complete the Resolve Issue section of the ROE RFS Action WI before completing the WI. When resolving the issue, the agent must review the ROE and the existing RODs. Comments in block 18 If a ROD is not found for the period of employment, the agent indicates that no decision matches the ROE when completing the Resolve Issue section of the ROE RFS Action WI. If there is a ROD on file for the period of employment, the agent completes the Resolve Issue section of the ROE RFS Action WI based on the ROD. Reviewing existing records of decision (RODs) on file If the ROD on file indicates that the RFS is labour dispute, the agent does not change the RFS in the Resolve Issue section of the ROE RFS Action WI. In this situation, the RFS on the ROE remains B - Strike or lockout . There is no Special Condition option when the RFS on the ROE is B - Strike or lockout . Note

      <title>Comments in block 18</title>
    </section>
    <p>If a ROD is not found for the period of employment, the agent indicates that no decision matches the ROE when completing the Resolve Issue section of the ROE RFS Action WI. If there is a ROD on file for the period of employment, the agent completes the Resolve Issue section of the ROE RFS Action WI based on the ROD.</p>
    <section>
      <title>Reviewing existing records of decision (RODs) on file</title>
    </section>
    <p>If the ROD on file indicates that the RFS is labour dispute, the agent does not change the RFS in the Resolve Issue section of the ROE RFS Action WI. In this situation, the RFS on the ROE remains B - Strike or lockout . There is no Special Condition option when the RFS on the ROE is B - Strike or lockout .</p>
    <xref href="../../common_notes/note_c_2675.dita">Note</xref>
  </conbody>
</concept>

Absent: CEGKMNS

1 document occurrences form 1 exact-content group(s): F. Exact means the expanded body, element order, attributes and link targets match after case/whitespace normalization; it does not establish equal applicability.

No corresponding node in C, E, G, K, M, N, S. Absence means absent from these maps, not absence of the policy.

Content example: “Note: The LDP is the final trip end date (fresh) or date of purchase (cured) on a fishing ROE.”

All eight publications are business-type task documents. Their RFS subject is inherited even when this section contains no category name. Exact text is a review opportunity, not permission to erase that scope.

30. What you need to look for / In RCMCEGMKNFS

8 document occurrences form 5 exact-content group(s): CEGM | K | N | F | S. Exact means the expanded body, element order, attributes and link targets match after case/whitespace normalization; it does not establish equal applicability.

Pair evidence: Exact content 6, Partial overlap 15, Related / unresolved 7. These labels are deterministic triage, not approved consolidation decisions.

Raw-body normalization hides expanded content or link/structure differences for C–N, E–N, G–N, M–N. Inspect the expanded text and source XML below.

C/E/G/M are structurally exact. N has the same checklist with a hyphen instead of an en dash in the Search / Correct screen name, so strict matching keeps it separate. K adds payroll-frequency, ownership, EI-request and service-provider categories. F asks about block 6B trip/purchase dates, block 11 RFS and block 12 comments; regular ROEs use blocks 11, 16 and 18. S contains only the first three questions. Reuse common questions individually while retaining these field and scope differences.

Observed shared passage (C–E): “Does the audit trail contain a new value for the RFS in block 16 ?”

All eight publications are business-type task documents. Their RFS subject is inherited even when this section contains no category name. Exact text is a review opportunity, not permission to erase that scope.

Inspect sources and all 28 within-row pairs

C · Linking the ROE and the matching period of employment · 387 tokens

en_EN/task/roe_rts/linking_the_roe_and_the_matching_period_f71580ff_non-complex.dita

Full expanded section

In NWS, on the Resolve Issue tab of the ROE RFS Action WI, determine if there is a matching period of employment already selected in the Matching POE list: If no , go to step 2. If yes, go to step 6. Determine if the Matching POE list includes one or more periods of employment with the same employer’s name as the ROE: If there are one or more periods of employment with the same employer’s name as the ROE, go to step 3. If there is no period of employment with the same employer’s name as the ROE, select POE not in the list . Click Save and go to step 6. Note In RCM, review the Search / Correct - Results screen to determine if the ROE in the ROE RFS Action WI is the one with the same employer’s name and the LDP closest to the LDW for one of the periods of employment on the Matching POE list in the ROE RFS Action WI in NWS: If the ROE is for the same employer and the LDP on the ROE is the best match to the LDW for one of the periods of employment, go to step 4. If the ROE is for the same employer but the LDP on the ROE is not the best match to the LDW for one of the periods of employment, go to step 5. If the ROE is not for the same employer or the LDP on the ROE is clearly not a good match to the LDW for one of the periods of employment, go to step 5. In NWS, on the Resolve Issue tab of the ROE RFS Action WI, complete the POE information section as follows: Matching POE: Select the period of employment with the same employer’s name and best match for LDP and LDW. Click Save . Go to step 6. In NWS, on the Resolve Issue tab of the ROE RFS Action WI, complete the POE information section as follows: Matching POE: Select POE not in the list . Click Save . Go to step 6. In NWS, on the Resolve Issue tab of the ROE RFS Action WI, determine if the Resolve Issue section is displayed: If the Resolve Issue section is displayed, go step 7. If the Resolve Issue section is not displayed, go to step 19.

            <li>Go to step 6.</li>

C · Ensuring the reason for separation (RFS) details are correct · 276 tokens

en_EN/task/roe_rts/ensuring_the_reason_for_separation_rfs__9dc9245b_non-complex.dita

Full expanded section

In RCM, review block 18 and the Additional information field of the ROE and determine if there are comments related to the RFS: If yes, go to step 8. If no , go to step 11. Note Determine if the ROE requires any of the following changes: If the comments on the ROE indicate: The RFS options in the ROE RFS Action WI must be changed as follows: RFS RFS sub-option apprenticeship training information J - Apprentice training not applicable return to school with apprenticeship training details (e.g. apprentice level or reference code) J - Apprentice training not applicable return to school with no apprenticeship training details E - Quit Quit - Return to school If the ROE requires a change to J - Apprentice training , go to step 9. If the ROE requires other changes, note the changes required and go to step 10. If the ROE does not require changes, go to step 11. Warning In NWS, determine if there is a ROD on file for the period of employment indicating that the RFS is other than apprentice training (e.g. dismissal or suspension, leave of absence, voluntary leaving): If no , go to step 10. If yes, go to step 11. In NWS, on the Resolve Issue tab of the ROE RFS Action WI, complete the Resolve Issue section as follows: Do the Reason for Separation details need to be modified?: Select Yes and the applicable RFS changes required (as indicated in step 8). Go to step 12. In NWS, on the Resolve Issue tab of the ROE RFS Action WI, complete the Resolve Issue section as follows: Do the Reason for Separation details need to be modified?: Select No . Go to step 12.

C · Selecting a special condition for the decision on file · 328 tokens

en_EN/task/roe_rts/selecting_a_special_condition_for_the_d_d5caef0c_non-complex.dita

Full expanded section

Determine which special condition options are displayed: If multiple options are displayed, go to step 13. If No Decision Matches the ROE is the only option displayed, go to step 17. If no special condition options are displayed, go to step 19. In NWS, determine if there is a ROD on file for the period of employment: If yes, go to step 14. If no , go to step 17. Review the decision indicated in the ROD: If the ROD indicates that adjudication was deferred, go to step 15. If the ROD does not indicate that adjudication was deferred, go to step 16. In NWS, on the Resolve Issue tab of the ROE RFS Action WI, complete the Resolve Issue section as follows: Select the Special Condition for the decision on file: Select Adjudication Deferred . Go to step 19. In RCM, review the ROE and determine if there is information which contradicts the decision on file (e.g. the decision on file has a different RFS than the ROE; the comments on file indicate apprentice training but the decision on file indicates something else): If there is contradictory information regarding the RFS on the ROE, go to step 17. If there is no contradictory information regarding the RFS on the ROE, go to step 18. In NWS, on the Resolve Issue tab of the ROE RFS Action WI, complete the Resolve issue section as follows: Select the Special Condition for the decision on file: Select No Decision Matches the ROE . Go to step 19. In NWS, on the Resolve Issue tab of the ROE RFS Action WI, complete the Resolve Issue section as follows: Select the Special Condition for the decision on file: Select the option that matches the decision in the ROD. If the decision indicates Suspension from employment (D-42) , select Suspension . If the decision indicates Anticipated Loss of Employment (D-44) , select Anticipated Loss of Employment and enter the disentitlement dates from the decision. Go to step 19.

E · Selecting a special condition for the decision on file · 329 tokens

en_EN/task/roe_quit/selecting_a_special_condition_for_the_d_d5caef0c_non-complex.dita

Full expanded section

Determine which special condition options are displayed: If multiple options are displayed, go to step 12. If No Decision Matches the ROE is the only option displayed, go to step 16. In NWS, determine if there is a ROD on file for the period of employment: If yes, go to step 13. If no , go to step 16. Review the decision indicated in the ROD: If the ROD indicates that adjudication was deferred, go to step 14. If the ROD does not indicate that adjudication was deferred, go to step 15. In NWS, on the Resolve Issue tab of the ROE RFS Action WI, complete the Resolve Issue section as follows: Select the Special Condition for the decision on file: Select Adjudication Deferred . Go to step 18. In RCM, review the ROE and determine if there is information which contradicts the decision on file (e.g. the decision on file has a different RFS than the ROE; the decision on file indicates voluntary leaving for another employment, but the comments from the employer on the ROE indicate that the employee quit for another reason): If there is contradictory information regarding the RFS on the ROE, go to step 16. If there is no contradictory information regarding the RFS on the ROE, go to step 17. In NWS, on the Resolve Issue tab of the ROE RFS Action WI, complete the Resolve Issue section as follows: Select the Special Condition for the decision on file: Select No Decision Matches the ROE . Go to step 18. In NWS, on the Resolve Issue tab of the ROE RFS Action WI, complete the Resolve Issue section as follows: Select the Special Condition for the decision on file: Select the option that matches the decision in the ROD. If the decision indicates Suspension from employment (D-42) , select Suspension . If the decision indicates Anticipated Loss of Employment (D-44) , select Anticipated Loss of Employment and enter the disentitlement dates from the decision. Go to step 18.

K · Selecting a special condition for the decision on file · 344 tokens

en_EN/task/roe_other/selecting_a_special_condition_for_the_d_d5caef0c_non-complex.dita

Full expanded section

Determine which special condition options are displayed: If multiple options are displayed, go to step 15. If No Decision Matches the ROE is the only option displayed, go to step 19. If no special condition options are displayed, go to step 21. In NWS, determine if there is a ROD on file for the period of employment: If yes, go to step 16. If no , go to step 19. Review the decision indicated in the ROD: If the ROD indicates that adjudication was deferred, go to step 17. If the ROD does not indicate that adjudication was deferred, go to step 18. In NWS, on the Resolve Issue tab of the ROE RFS Action WI, complete the Resolve Issue section as follows: Select the Special Condition for the decision on file: Select Adjudication Deferred . Go to step 21. In RCM, review the ROE and determine if there is information which contradicts the decision on file (e.g. the decision on file has a different RFS than the ROE; the decision on file indicates voluntary leaving for another employment, but the comments from the employer on the ROE indicate that the employee quit due to a conflict with a manager): If there is contradictory information regarding the RFS on the ROE, go to step 19. If there is no contradictory information regarding the RFS on the ROE, go to step 20. In NWS, on the Resolve Issue tab of the ROE RFS Action WI, complete the Resolve Issue section as follows: Select the Special Condition for the decision on file: Select No Decision Matches the ROE . Go to step 21. In NWS, on the Resolve Issue tab of the ROE RFS Action WI, complete the Resolve Issue section as follows: Select the Special Condition for the decision on file: Select the option that matches the decision in the ROD: If the decision indicates Suspension from employment (D-42) , select Suspension . If the decision indicates Anticipated Loss of Employment (D-44) , select Anticipated Loss of Employment and enter the disentitlement dates from the decision. Go to step 21.

M · Selecting a special condition for the decision on file · 447 tokens

en_EN/task/roe_dismissal/selecting_a_special_condition_for_the_d_d5caef0c_non-complex.dita

Full expanded section

Determine which special condition options are displayed: If multiple options are displayed, go to step 12. If No Decision Matches the ROE is the only option displayed, go to step 18. In NWS, determine if there is a ROD on file for the period of employment: If yes, go to step 13. If no , go to step 18. Review the decision indicated in the ROD: If the ROD indicates that adjudication was deferred, go to step 14. If the ROD does not indicate that adjudication was deferred, go to step 15. In NWS, on the Resolve Issue tab of the ROE RFS Action WI, complete the Resolve Issue section as follows: Select the Special Condition for the decision on file: Select Adjudication Deferred . Go to step 21. Review the decision indicated in the ROD: If the ROD indicates Dismissal — ROE confirmation required , go to step 16. If the ROD does not indicate “Dismissal — ROE confirmation required”, go to step 17. In RCM, review the ROE and determine if the comments on the ROE confirm the automated decision on file (i.e. the comments on the ROE indicate that the client was dismissed for unsuitability or did not pass the probationary period or was terminated during the probationary period): If the comments on the ROE do not confirm the automated decision, go to step 18. If the comments on the ROE confirm the automated decision, go to step 19. In RCM, review the ROE and determine if there is information which contradicts the decision on file (e.g. the decision on file has a different RFS than the ROE): If there is contradictory information regarding the RFS on the ROE, go to step 18. If there is no contradictory information regarding the RFS on the ROE, go to step 20. In NWS, on the Resolve Issue tab of the ROE RFS Action WI, complete the Resolve Issue section as follows: Select the Special Condition for the decision on file: Select No Decision Matches the ROE . Go to step 21. In NWS, on the Resolve Issue tab of the ROE RFS Action WI, complete the Resolve Issue section as follows: Select the Special Condition for the decision on file: Select Misconduct – Not Proven . Go to step 21. In NWS, on the Resolve Issue tab of the ROE RFS Action WI, complete the Resolve Issue section as follows: Select the Special Condition for the decision on file: Select the option that matches the decision in the ROD. If the decision indicates Suspension from employment (D-42) , select Suspension . If the decision indicates Anticipated Loss of Employment (D-44) , select Anticipated Loss of Employment and enter the disentitlement dates from the decision. Go to step 21.

Inspect sources and all 21 within-row pairs
C · Note · 89 tokens

en_EN/common_notes/note_c_2685.dita

Full expanded section

Note: When comparing the employer’s name on the period of employment and the ROE, you may accept minor spelling differences to the employer’s name. You must also consider that the employer may do business under more than one name (e.g. 123456 ONT INC does business as ABC Company). In this case, you must review the documentation on file or use the Employer search (by business number) in FTS to determine if the employer’s name on the ROE is equivalent to the employer’s name on the period of employment.

Original XML

<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<!DOCTYPE concept PUBLIC "-//OASIS//DTD DITA 2.0 Concept//EN" "concept.dtd">
<concept id="note_c_2685">
  <title>Note</title>
  <conbody>
    <p>Note:</p>
    <p>When comparing the employer’s name on the period of employment and the ROE, you may accept minor spelling differences to the employer’s name. You must also consider that the employer may do business under more than one name (e.g. 123456 ONT INC does business as ABC Company). In this case, you must review the documentation on file or use the Employer search (by business number) in FTS to determine if the employer’s name on the ROE is equivalent to the employer’s name on the period of employment.</p>
  </conbody>
</concept>
E · Note · 89 tokens

en_EN/common_notes/note_c_2685.dita

Full expanded section

Note: When comparing the employer’s name on the period of employment and the ROE, you may accept minor spelling differences to the employer’s name. You must also consider that the employer may do business under more than one name (e.g. 123456 ONT INC does business as ABC Company). In this case, you must review the documentation on file or use the Employer search (by business number) in FTS to determine if the employer’s name on the ROE is equivalent to the employer’s name on the period of employment.
